Microsoft [HttpCompletionOption documentation](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/dotnet/api/system.net.http.httpcompletionoption?view=net-10.0) explicitly states HttpClient timeout with ResponseHeadersRead covers HEADERS ONLY, and response BODY requires its own timeout.
